- **doc-writer**: Technical documentation writer crystallized from studying how Stripe/Linear-quality doc practitioners think. Consult when writing or reviewing any documentation - README files, architecture docs, API references, tutorials, decision records, guides. Trigger conditions: "write docs for," "document this," "README," "architecture doc," "how should I explain," "this needs documentation," reviewing existing docs for quality.
